- [`UiKeyboardFocus_SelectInitial`](../../src/ui/controls/input.cpp#L263) - Gives the keyboard focus to the first node from root on that is a preferred focus target, else to the last fallback focus target found (suppressed nodes are skipped); without any the focus stays as ...
- [`UiKeyboardFocus_AcquireIfNone`](../../src/ui/controls/input.cpp#L293) - Called when a control is unsuppressed or its page becomes active: gives it the keyboard focus if no node holds the focus yet and the control is a focus target.
- [`UiNode_DefaultPointerMove`](../../src/ui/controls/input.cpp#L305) - Default pointerMove slot of most UI vtables (range sliders, labels, lists, ...): the node asks for cursor frame 0 (GRAPHICS_CURSOR_FRAME_ARROW).
- [`UiKeyboardFocus_MoveNext`](../../src/ui/controls/input.cpp#L516) - Moves the keyboard focus to the next focus target after the current one in depth-first tree order, wrapping around through the topmost ancestor and skipping suppressed nodes.
- [`UiNode_ForwardPointerWheelToParent`](../../src/ui/controls/input.cpp#L599) - Default pointerWheel slot of most UI vtables: passes the wheel event up to the parent node (if any), so it reaches the nearest ancestor that handles the wheel.
- [`UiNode_DefaultKeyboardEventMoveFocusNext`](../../src/ui/controls/input.cpp#L616) - Default keyboardEvent slot of many UI vtables, also the fallback of the slider and focus-proxy handlers.
- [`UiKeyboardFocus_Set`](../../src/ui/controls/input.cpp#L626) - Moves the keyboard focus to node (UI_NODE_NONE clears it), keeping UI_NODE_HAS_KEYBOARD_FOCUS on the focused node only, and redraws every root (also when the focus did not change).

#### `node_views.h`

[Header](../../include/thandor/ui/controls/node_views.h)

The UI control types as prefixed views of their base node (core/slot.h THANDOR_SLOT_PREFIX): a vtable slot taking a UiNodeBase * (or UiSelectableControl *, ...) accepts a function taking the control type. Each control starts with its base struct at offset 0 (checked by the registration).
